act_commands3.py << 'EOF'
> import re
>
> with open('video.mp4', 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
>     content = f.read()
> # Remove HTML tags
> content = re.sub(r'<[^>]*>', '', content)
> # Split by newline
> lines = content.split('\n')
> commands = []
> for line in lines:
>     line = line.strip()
>     # Remove any leading/trailing non-alphanumeric characters
>     # We'll look for lines that contain at least one letter and maybe a digit, and are not too long
>     if re.search(r'[a-zA-Z]', line) and len(line) < 200:
>         # Also, we want to capture lines that start with a letter and are not JavaScript
>         # We'll also check for common zork command patterns
>         # We'll add a simple regex for common commands
>         if re.match(r'^[a-zA-Z][a-zA-Z0-9 ]*$', line):
>             commands.append(line)
>         # Also, we want to capture lines that are exactly the commands from the video
>         # Let's also try to find lines that contain letters and are not too long and not containing 'window' or 'yt'
>         # We'll filter out any lines that contain common JavaScript patterns
> javascript_keywords = ['window', 'document', 'function', 'var', 'let', 'const', 'class', 'import', 'export', 'try', 'catch', 'throw', 'if', 'else', 'for', 'wh
ile', 'do', 'switch', 'case', 'break', 'continue', 'return', 'new', 'delete', 'this', 'super', 'self', 'Base', 'Polymer', 'yt', 'YouTube']
> filtered = []
> for cmd in commands:
>     if not any(keyword in cmd for keyword in javascript_keywords):
>         filtered.append(cmd)
> # Write to solution.txt
> with open('/app/solution.txt', 'w') as f:
>     for cmd in filtered:
>         f.write(cmd + '\n')
